# Macro names in metrics/out/numbers.tex (written by recompute.py)

Every macro is `\newcommand{\<name>}{<value>}`. Names follow the draft pipeline (`make_tables.macro_name`): the underscore key is title-cased, non-alphanumerics are dropped, the prefix `num` is added and digits are replaced letter by letter with `str.maketrans('0123456789', 'ZABCDEFGHI')` (`pillar0` -> `PillarZ`, `rsna2019_ich` -> `RsnaBZAIIch`, `joint_8k` -> `JointHK`). Exceptions without the `num` prefix: `\pooled<bb><method>` (method without underscores) and the draft's `\numRunsCls`, `\numLabels`, `\numDelongTests`, `\numSegRunsN`.

Formatting (from the key): `*meandiff*`, `*delta*` and keys ending in `diff` are signed with three decimals (`+0.034`; a value that rounds to zero is `0.000`); keys containing `better` or `worse`, ending in `_n`, `_sig`, `_ntest`, `_hit`, or starting with `cohort_` / `bestrows_` are integers (percentages rounded to whole numbers); `rank_*_mean`, `segrank_*_mean`, `vocorank_*_mean`, `vocosegrank_*_mean`, `rankall_*_mean`, `segrankall_*_mean`, `*_spearman` and `gaincorr` have two decimals; `segrecomp_*diff` four; everything else three. `bestarm_*`, `segbestarm_*` and `fix_<bb>_chosen` are method names (LaTeX). Macros whose value is undefined (no runs) are not written.

Backbone tokens: tapct, spectre, pillar0, voco, all (= pooled over tapct, spectre, pillar0). Cohort tokens: amos_covid_luna, fdg, rsna2022, rsna2023, rsna2019_ich; datasets amos22, kits23, lits, msd_pancreas, totalseg. Mode tokens: pertask, joint, joint_lm (26 000-step classification joint), joint_8k / jointeightk (8000-step joint of AMOS22 / TotalSegmentator), pertask_cm.

## Families

- `pooled<bb><method>`: held-out AUROC pooled over labels and the per-task and joint modes, per backbone and method (no num prefix)
- `spread_<bb>_<method>`: median over label-mode pairs of the SD of the held-out probabilities; spread_othermin = smallest value among pairs that trained; spread_min_value = threshold; dnt_cells_n = label-mode rows of pairs that did not train
- `cls_auroc_<cohort>_<bb>_<mode>_<method>`: mean held-out AUROC over the labels of a cohort (complete cells; modes pertask, joint, joint_lm)
- `seg_<dataset>_<bb>_<mode>_<method>`: mean held-out Dice over the structures of a dataset (complete cells; modes pertask, joint)
- `segbudget_<dataset>_<bb>_<pertask|joint_8k|joint>_<method>`: AMOS22 / TotalSegmentator: per-structure 8000-step, joint 8000-step and joint 24000-step means
- `paired_<bb>_<baseline>_<method>_{meandiff,better,sigbetter,sigworse,n}`: DeLong comparisons per label-mode pair: mean difference, % pairs with the method ahead, % BH-significant (p_bh < 0.05) ahead / behind, number of pairs
- `head_<bb>_*`: headline row per backbone: n = label-mode pairs of Lin; LoRA vs Lin and vs Full FT (mean difference, % ahead, % significant); pooled AUROC per method
- `rank_*, near_*, win_*, regret_*, regretmax_*, bestarm_*`: classification ranks: rank_slots_n / rank_<bb>_slots_n slots; rank_<bb>_<method>_mean and rank_<method>_mean (pooled over main backbones); near = % of slots within 0.01 of the best; win = % of slots best; regret = mean AUROC deficit to the best; regretmax = largest deficit; bestarm = method with the lowest mean rank (name)
- `segrank_*, segbestarm_*`: segmentation ranks over structure-mode slots with all seven methods, per backbone and pooled
- `vs<ref>_<bb|all>_<method>_{meandiff,better,n}`: method minus reference (lora, lorag, babmil, fullft) on the same backbone x cohort x label x mode, pairs that trained
- `fullftsens_tapct_{all,nofdgjoint}_meandiff`: Full FT minus Lin on TAP-CT at label level, with and without the joint FDG run
- `segpaired_<bb>_<baseline>_<method>_{meandiff,better,sigbetter,sigworse,n}; segpairedmode_..._<mode>_{meandiff,n}`: paired-bootstrap comparisons per structure-mode pair (BH within baseline family)
- `segjoint_*, segjointds_*, segcollapse_*, segjointsmall_min_meandiff, seg_runs_n`: joint minus per-structure Dice per backbone, method and dataset; collapsed structures (joint < 0.01 while per-structure >= 0.4) and the means without them
- `segjointeightk_<bb>_meandiff, segbudgetgain_<bb>_meandiff`: AMOS22/TotalSegmentator: 8000-step joint minus per-structure; 24000-step joint minus 8000-step joint
- `segmode_<dataset>_<bb>_<mode>_<lora|fullft>_meandiff, segmodepos_<bb>_<method>_<mode>_n, segmodecells_...`: LoRA minus Dec and Full FT minus Dec per dataset, backbone and mode (joint_8k written jointeightk); number of positive cells
- `seggap_<method>_*, segdelta_<bb>_<baseline>_<method>_*, segdeltamode_*, segvslora_*`: SPECTRE minus TAP-CT Dice per method; method minus Lin/Dec per structure-mode pair (mean, % ahead, median, min, drops below -0.1 and the mean without them); method minus LoRA
- `clshead_*`: attentive minus linear probe (classification) per label-mode pair
- `conv_*`: share of runs (main backbones, seed 42, two-stage methods excluded) whose selected checkpoint is the last validation (last_better) or one of the last two (late_better); conv_seg_<pertask|joint|jointlong|jointeightk>_runs_n = runs counted
- `ap_*`: LoRA minus Lin in held-out average precision per label-mode pair (pairs that trained); rare = held-out prevalence below 10%
- `ci_<key>_<bb|all>_{meandiff,lodiff,hidiff,lotwodiff,hitwodiff,pos_n,clusters_n,labels_n}`: label-level estimate: mean over labels, three-stage interval (lo/hi), two-stage interval (lotwo/hitwo), cohort-backbone clusters with a positive mean and their number, labels. Keys: the draft's names (clsloralin, clsattnftlora, clsfullftlin, clsjoint, segdeclin, segloradec, segloradecpt, segloradecjt, segfullftdec, segjoint) and the new keys without underscores (e.g. clsattnlin, clsloraglin, seglorageddec)
- `voco_*, vocorank_*, vocosegrank_*, vocovslin_*, vocopaired_*`: VoCo: run counts, ranks over label-mode slots with all methods, every method minus Lin at label level (per cohort too), DeLong summaries
- `rankall_*, segrankall_*`: mean rank pooled over the slots of all four backbones (VoCo included): rankall_slots_n / segrankall_slots_n slots, rankall_<method>_mean / segrankall_<method>_mean; slots and per-slot ranks as rank_* / segrank_* / vocorank_* / vocosegrank_*
- `jointdelta_*, jointlmdelta_*, jointcohort_*, jointpairs_n, jointneg_n`: joint minus per-task AUROC over (method, label) pairs: pooled, per backbone, per backbone and cohort (jointlm: 26000-step joint), per cohort and method; number of (backbone, cohort) pairs and how many are negative
- `jointlong_<bb>_<method>_meandiff, jointlong_<bb>_{min,max}diff`: CVPR26: 26000-step joint minus 8000-step joint AUROC, mean over labels, per backbone and method (Table tab:ladder), and its range over methods per backbone
- `sigcohort_<cohort>_*, sigbysize_<small|mid|large>_*`: LoRA vs Lin DeLong rows (pairs that trained) by cohort (% significant ahead, % ahead, mean difference, median held-out n) and by held-out size (<100, 100-400, >=400)
- `bestrows_*`: how often each method has the highest cohort mean of a (cohort, backbone, mode) row (pairs that trained; modes pertask, joint, joint_lm)
- `gainbin_<low|mid|high|top>_*, gainbinattn_*, gaincorr`: LoRA (attn) minus Lin by the linear probe's selection-split AUROC bin (<=0.6, 0.6-0.7, 0.7-0.8, >0.8); Spearman correlation of probe AUROC and LoRA gain
- `bbdiff_*`: SPECTRE minus TAP-CT held-out AUROC per shared cohort and method (modes pooled; pairs that trained)
- `select_*, valtest_<cohort>_spearman`: per slot (cohort, backbone, mode, label) with >= 4 methods: is the method with the best selection-split AUROC also best held-out (hit, %), its held-out deficit (regret), the deficit of always choosing LoRA, and the Spearman correlation of selection and held-out AUROC per cohort
- `early_<bb>_<method>_better, early_<bb>_<method>_median_n`: % of runs selected at <= 2000 iterations and the median selected iteration (classification, main backbones, seed 42, every mode)
- `cohortmode_<bb>_<cohort>_<mode>_<method>_{meandiff,n}, cohortall_<bb>_<cohort>_<method>_meandiff, worstcohort_*`: mean gain over Lin (DeLong rows, pairs that trained) per backbone, cohort, mode and method; per backbone, cohort and method; the worst (backbone, cohort) mean per method and over every method but Full FT
- `secondorder_max`: largest |method minus LoRA| among the head and schedule variants (not Full FT, not Attn), and |Attn minus Lin|
- `seeds<cls|seg>_<a>_<b>_<bb|all>_{pos_n,pairs_n,extrapos_n,extrapairs_n}, seeds<task>_<method>_{sdmedian,sdmax,cells_n}, seeds<task>_runs_n`: seed repeats of the joint models: (cohort, seed) pairs with A > B (all seeds; seeds 43/44 only), SD of the cohort mean across seeds (median and max over cells with >= 2 seeds), number of extra-seed runs
- `fix_<bb>_*, fixsel_<bb>_*`: full fine-tuning settings (lower LR, layer decay, accumulation + clipping): mean selection-split AUROC, cohorts whose run trained (spread >= 0.01), the setting chosen per backbone (highest selection AUROC) and its label-level difference to Lin and LoRA
- `ctrl_<bb>_<a>_<b>_{meandiff,pos_n,cohorts_n}`: controls: MLP-only FT vs Attn-only FT and LoRA; rsLoRA at LR/4 vs LoRA and rsLoRA (label-level differences of the joint models; cohorts with a positive mean)
- `cm_<dataset>_<bb>_<method>_{jointdiff,pertaskdiff,pos_n,struct_n}`: compute-matched per-structure models (1600 steps): joint minus matched and 8000-step per-structure minus matched, structures with the joint model ahead
- `scratch_<dataset>_<bb>_{meandiff,mean}`: from-scratch joint models (TAP-CT architecture, random init) minus the Full FT joint model, mean over structures
- `runs_<task>_<bb>_n, runs_<task>_n, labels_<bb>_n, structs_<bb>_n, labels_cls_n, structs_seg_n, delong_tests_n, segpaired_tests_n, numRunsCls, numLabels, numDelongTests, numSegRunsN`: counts: runs used per task and backbone (main design, seed 42, main methods, no breast MRI cohort), labels / structures per backbone, DeLong and paired-bootstrap comparisons; the draft's names numRunsCls (main cls runs without VoCo), numLabels (distinct cohort-label pairs), numDelongTests (vs Lin and Full FT, main backbones, pairs that trained), numSegRunsN
- `cohort_<cohort>_<val|test>, cohort_<cohort>_<split>_ids_n, cases_<dataset>_<split>_n`: held-out and selection cases per cohort from the per-case files (rows of the joint Lin run; FDG rows are studies, ids are patients; CVPR26 split into amos / covid / luna by label source); segmentation cases per dataset and split
- `segrecomp_*, selcheck_<task>_*`: agreement of the recomputed selection-split macro metric with the logged best value: segrecomp over the segmentation runs whose per-case file was re-inferred at the selected checkpoint (single per-case folder), selcheck over every run used
